is “to promote a prosperous Hong Kong where companies and 實現並享受到高效數碼化帶來的效益」，這是

(4) Our Values (4) 我們的核心價值
Guiding us in everything we do, our values are set out below: 引領我們一言一行的核心價值載列如下：
Trust We work diligently to uphold our reputation for helping organisations maximise
their digital efficiency and successfully get more value from technology.
可靠務實 我們的聲譽奠基於我們一直努力不懈地協助企業充分發揮數碼化效益，並成功在科
技上取得最大回報。
Transparency We are open and direct in the way we communicate and conduct our business.
We encourage information sharing across the Company. We own up to our
mistakes, work to correct them and learn from them.
公開透明 我們以公開透明的方式溝通和經營業務。我們鼓勵公司內部資料共享。我們勇於承
擔，不斷致力改善，並從錯誤中學習。
Teamwork We are committed to close teamwork among ourselves, with our business
partners and customers, with regulators and others we work with.
團隊精神 我們致力於各部門，並與商業合作夥伴、客戶，以至監管機構及其他工作夥伴，建
立緊密的合作關係。
Thoughtfulness We give our team members the resources and support to understand the pain
points of our customers and offer practical solutions to overcome them. We
cultivate empathy and care for each other, our customers and all stakeholders
whose lives we touch.
誠懇關懷 我們為同事提供資源和支援，以了解客戶的問題所在，從而提供實用的解決方案。
我們培養同理心，互相關懷，並照顧客戶和持份者所需。
Technology Our role is to maximise digital efficiency with a science-based approach that
involves measuring performance and constantly seeking improvement. We
will continue to explore innovative new technology that can help raise digital
efficiency in Hong Kong.
科技為本 我們以科技為本的方法，量化成效，不斷尋求進步空間，以充分發揮數碼化效益。
我們將繼續研究創新科技，幫助香港提高數碼化效益。
Timely We respect people’s time. From developing effective e-solutions to responding
swiftly to customer requests, we seek to make best use of time. Working
with a sense of timeliness helps us meet deadlines and keeps us ahead of
technological change.
與時並進 我們珍惜時間。從發展有效電子方案，至迅速回應客戶需求，我們務求在有限的時
間內達成最大的效益。有效管理時間有助我們按時完成工作，並在日新月異的科技
中保持優勢。

About This Report 關於本報告
This Environmental, Social and Governance Report is made by the 本環境、社會及管治報告由本公司編製，以向各持
Company to disclose and demonstrate the Group’s performance 份者披露並展示本集團應對重要關注事宜方面的環
in ESG in response to material issues of concern to various 境、社會及管治表現。
stakeholders.
Reporting Scope and Boundary 匯報範圍及界限
The reporting boundary of this report covers the Company and all 本報告的匯報界限涵蓋本公司及其所有附屬公司於
its subsidiaries for the period from 1 January 2025 to 31 December 二零二五年一月一日至二零二五年十二月三十一日
2025 (the “Reporting Period”). Same as the reporting scope 期間（「報告期間」）的相關情況。與二零二四年為提
extended in 2024 to enhance the comprehensiveness and accuracy 高環境、社會及管治披露的全面性及準確性而擴大
of our ESG disclosures, we have incorporated all our subsidiaries, 的匯報範圍相同，我們已將所有附屬公司（包括位於
including those located outside Hong Kong. We target to maintain 香港以外的附屬公司）納入匯報範圍。我們計劃在
this reporting scope for the ESG Reports in the future to ensure 日後的環境、社會及管治報告中維持該匯報範圍，
continuity and clarity in our commitment to ESG practices. 以確保我們對環境、社會及管治實踐的承諾的持續
性及清晰度。

Governance (Continued)
Materiality Assessment
To evaluate the materiality of ESG issues that are significant to our
investors and stakeholders, the Company considers the reporting
principles outlined in the ESG Code, the nature and core activities
of its businesses (including risks to its businesses) and the
outcomes of stakeholders’ engagement. Based on this materiality
assessment, we will periodically review and adjust our ESG strategy
as needed to ensure it remains aligned with stakeholders’ priorities
and evolving business needs.
Identification: Based on the market trends, standards, media
coverage, benchmarking analysis and insights, fourteen ESG-related
issues were identified.
Prioritisation: The topics to be reported were determined and
prioritised from two dimensions, namely materiality to the Group’s
business and materiality to the stakeholders.
Validation: Regarding the stakeholders’ feedback and analysis, five
issues were identified as “high materiality”. The result was verified
by the Board.

重要性評估
為評估對投資者及持份者而言屬重要的環境、社會
及管治事宜的重要性，本公司會考慮環境、社會及
管治守則所概述的匯報原則、其業務的性質及核心
活動（包括其業務風險），以及持份者參與的結果。
我們根據該重要性評估定期檢討並按需要調整我們
的環境、社會及管治策略，以確保其與持份者優先
考量事項及不斷演變的業務需求保持一致。
識別：根據市場趨勢、標準、媒體報道、基準分析
及見解，識別出十四項環境、社會及管治相關事宜。
優次排列：從對本集團業務的重要性及對持份者的
重要性兩個維度釐定須予匯報的議題，並對該等議
題進行優次排列。
驗證：有關持份者的反饋及分析方面，識別出五項
「高度重要性」事宜。該結果經董事會核實。

unnecessary energy consumption, the Group has implemented a

Lighting and Electrical Systems • Replacing traditional lighting with LED fixtures; and

• 以LED燈具替換傳統照明；及

risks and opportunities.

for oversight of climate-related risks and opportunities, integrated 者，本集團已建立明確的四階式管治架構，負責監

report.

background and experience, providing specialised insight into 討論中，為可持續發展及氣候相關事宜提供專業見

and opportunities, generally based on qualitative factors, on a 風險及機遇連同其他類型的風險均會根據本報

Metrics and Targets 指標及目標
The Group has adopted a unified climate-related target that 本集團已採納統一的氣候相關目標，將營運脫碳與
combines operational decarbonisation with value chain 價值鏈賦能相結合，完全符合《香港氣候行動藍圖
enablement, fully aligned with Hong Kong’s Climate Action Plan 2050》及《巴黎協定》提出的1.5°C控溫路徑。該目標
2050 and the Paris Agreement’s 1.5°C pathway. The target takes 以二零二四年為基準年，涵蓋範圍1、範圍2及範圍
2024 as the baseline, covering Scope 1, Scope 2 and Scope 3 3溫室氣體排放。本集團將每年檢討該目標，通過
GHG emissions. The target will be reviewed annually, tracked 能源記錄、賬單及客戶反饋等方式進行追蹤，任何
through energy records, bills and customer feedback, etc., with any 修訂將根據進展評估及不斷演變的業務情況在日後
revisions documented and explained in future ESG Reports based 的環境、社會及管治報告中予以記錄及說明。該目
on progress assessments and evolving business circumstances. 標及其基礎方法尚未經過第三方驗證。
The target and its underlying methodology have not been validated
by a third party.
For short-term target, by 2028, we will focus on building strong 就短期目標而言，於二零二八年前，我們將專注建
internal foundations. This includes conducting comprehensive 構內部基礎。其包括對內部範圍3溫室氣體排放進行
accounting and statistical analysis of our internal Scope 3 GHG 全面的會計及統計分析，以更深入地了解其對價值
emissions to gain a deeper understanding of value-chain impacts, 鏈的影響，同時持續提升員工的可持續發展意識。
alongside continuously enhancing employees' sustainability 於報告期間，本集團溫室氣體排放密度為每名僱員
awareness. For the Reporting Period, the Group’s GHG emissions 1.44噸二氧化碳當量，較基準年略有減少。然而，
intensity was 1.44 tCO₂-e per employee, reflecting a slight decrease 本集團範圍3溫室氣體排放由4.72噸二氧化碳當量
compared with the baseline. However, the Group’s Scope 3 GHG 增加至10.68噸二氧化碳當量，乃歸因於其範圍3溫
emissions increased from 4.72 tCO₂-e to 10.68 tCO₂-e, attributing to 室氣體排放數據收集能力有所提升。
its enhanced Scope 3 GHG emissions data collection.

challenges faced by SMEs and emphasised how Tradelink’s 點介紹貿易通透過其綠色供應鏈應用方案對可持續
technological solutions can help them unlock greater value and 發展的承諾，該應用方案旨在支援企業在提升供應
efficiency. The session also highlighted Tradelink’s commitment 鏈透明度及可追溯性的同時，實現其環境、社會及
to sustainability through its green SCS, which aims to support 管治目標。我們亦就數碼身份及簽署、人工智能驅
businesses in achieving their ESG goals while improving 動網絡安全、數碼開戶及加密創新等關鍵議題分享
transparency and traceability across the supply chain. We also 見解。
shared insights on key topics including digital identity and signing,
AI-driven cybersecurity, digital onboarding and crypto innovation.
Case: ESG Pledge Seminar organised by the Chinese 案例：香港中華廠商聯合會（「廠商會」）舉辦的
Manufacturers’ Association of Hong Kong (“CMA”) ESG約章講座
During the Reporting Period, Tradelink was honoured to participate 於報告期間，貿易通很榮幸參與由廠商會舉辦的
in the ESG Pledge Seminar organised by CMA. We shared our ESG約章講座。我們分享有關將環境、社會及管治
insights and experiences on integrating ESG principles into 原則整合至業務營運的見解及經驗，重點關注如何
business operations, focusing on supporting their sustainability 支持企業的可持續發展歷程。我們亦探討數碼簽署
journey. We also discussed the role of digital signing in promoting 在促進環境、社會及管治方面的角色，強調採用經
ESG, emphasising the importance of adopting secure and compliant 由可靠第三方驗證及╱或簽發的數碼證書所支援的
digital signatures backed by digital certificates validated and/or 安全合規數碼簽署的重要性，因為其有助於加強管
issued by a trusted third party, as it can help enhance governance, 治、確保數據完整性並與持份者建立信任。該研討
ensure data integrity and build trust with stakeholders. The seminar 會吸引逾100名參加者，匯聚致力於可持續發展的
attracted over 100 participants, bringing together industry leaders 行業領袖及專業人士。
and professionals dedicated to sustainable development.

Directors’ Report (Continued)
董事會報告書（續）
Share Option Scheme 購股權計劃
The Share Option Scheme 2014 was adopted on 9 May 2014 and 二零一四年購股權計劃於二零一四年五月九日獲採
expired on 8 May 2024, upon which no further options shall be 納並於二零二四年五月八日屆滿，自此概無任何購
granted under the Share Option Scheme 2014. The Board decided 股權將根據二零一四年購股權計劃獲授出。董事會
at the Board meeting held on 22 March 2024 that no new share 於二零二四年三月二十二日舉行的董事會會議上決
option scheme would be adopted, however, the outstanding options 定不採納任何新購股權計劃，然而，根據二零一四
granted under the Share Option Scheme 2014 shall continue to be 年購股權計劃授出的尚未行使的購股權將維持有效
valid and exercisable up to the end of the relevant exercise periods. 及可行使，直至有關行使期完結為止。

Credit-impaired financial assets 信貸減值的財務資產
At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether 於各報告日期，本集團評估財務資
a financial asset is credit-impaired. A financial asset 產有否出現信貸減值。當發生一項
is credit-impaired when one or more events that 或多項對財務資產估計未來現金流
have a detrimental impact on the estimated future 產生不利影響的事件時，財務資產
cash flows of the financial asset have occurred. 即被視為出現信貸減值。
Evidence that a financial asset is credit-impaired 財務資產出現信貸減值的證據包括
includes the following observable events: 以下可觀察事件：
– significant financial difficulties of the debtor; – 債務人面對重大財務困難；
– a breach of contract, such as a default or – 違反合約，例如拖欠或逾期償
delinquency in interest or principal payments; 還利息或本金
– the restructuring of a loan or advance by the – 本集團根據在其他情況下不會
Group on terms that the Group would not 考慮的條款重組貸款或墊款；
consider otherwise;
– it is probable that the debtor will enter – 債務人有可能破產或進行其他
bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation; or 財務重組；或
– the disappearance of an active market for a – 發行人陷入財困導致證券失去
security because of financial difficulties of the 活躍市場。
issuer.

Hong Kong employees that have been employed continuously 根據香港《僱傭條例》，連續受僱至少五年的香
for at least five years are entitled to long service payments in 港僱員在若干情況下有權享有長服金。該等情
accordance with the Hong Kong Employment Ordinance under 況包括：僱員因嚴重不當行為或裁員以外的原
certain circumstances. These circumstances include where 因而被解僱；僱員於65歲或以上的年齡辭任；
an employee is dismissed for reasons other than serious 或僱傭合約有固定年期且屆滿後不予重續。長
misconduct or redundancy, that employee resigns at the age 服金應付款項的金額乃參考僱員的最終薪金
of 65 or above, or the employment contract is of fixed term （上限為港幣22,500元）及服務年限釐定，並扣
and expires without renewal. The amount of LSP payable 除集團向強積金計劃供款（見附註20）所產生
is determined with reference to the employee’s final salary 的任何應計福利金額，整體上限為每名僱員港
(capped at HK$22,500) and the years of service, reduced by 幣390,000元。目前，本集團並無任何獨立資
the amount of any accrued benefits derived from the Group’s 金安排以履行其長服金責任。
contributions to MPF scheme (see Note 20), with an overall
cap of HK$390,000 per employee. Currently, the Group does
not have any separate funding arrangement in place to meet
its LSP obligation.

Retirement Schemes Legislation (Offsetting Arrangement) 傭及退休計劃法例（抵銷安排）（修訂）條例》
(Amendment) Ordinance 2022 (the “2022 Amendment （「2022年修訂條例」）生效，該條例取消了僱主
Ordinance”) came into effect, which abolishes the statutory 可透過動用其對強制性積金計劃的強制性供款
right of an employer to reduce its LSP payable to a Hong 來減少應付予香港僱員的長服金的法定權利。
Kong employee by drawing on its mandatory contributions 此外，一項為期25年的計劃已於二零二五年五
to the MPF scheme. Separately, a 25-year scheme to provide 月一日起實施，旨在為僱主因長服金過渡後部
a subsidy (“Subsidy”) for employers’ costs in relation to the 分產生的費用提供補貼（「補貼」）。
post-transition portion of the LSP has been implemented with

Among other things, upon the abolition of the offsetting 其中，一旦取消抵銷機制生效，僱主自過渡日
mechanism takes effect, an employer can no longer use 期起不得再使用其強制性強積金計劃供款所
any of the accrued benefits derived from its mandatory 產生的任何應計利益（不論於過渡日期之前、

20 Employee retirement benefits 20 僱員退休福利
The Group operates a Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme (the 本集團根據《香港強制性公積金計劃條例》，為
“MPF scheme”) under the Hong Kong Mandatory Provident 根據香港《僱傭條例》僱用的僱員，設立強制性
Fund Schemes Ordinance for employees employed under 公積金計劃（「強積金計劃」）。強積金計劃為獨
the jurisdiction of the Hong Kong Employment Ordinance. 立受託人管理的界定供款退休計劃。根據強積
The MPF scheme is a defined contribution retirement plan 金計劃，僱主與僱員須各自向計劃作出相等於
administered by independent trustees. Under the MPF scheme, 僱員有關入息5%的供款，而有關入息以每月港
the employer and its employees are each required to make 幣30,000元為上限。本集團向計劃作出的供款
contributions to the plan at 5% of the employees’ relevant 即時歸屬有關僱員。
income, subject to a cap of monthly relevant income of
HK$30,000. Contributions to the plan vest immediately.

Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency risk 在本集團的日常業務過程中，會遇上信貸、流
arises in the normal course of the Group’s business. These 動資金、利率及外匯風險。該等風險受到本集
risks are limited by the Group’s financial management policies 團於下文載述的財務管理政策及常規所限制。
and practices described below.
(a) Credit risk (a) 信貸風險
Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will 信貸風險指對手方將違反合約義務導致本
default on its contractual obligations resulting in a 集團蒙受財務虧損的風險。本集團的信貸
financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk is 風險主要源自應收賬款及合約資產。由於
primarily attributable to trade receivables and contract 對手方為主要銀行，本集團面臨現金及現
assets. The Group’s exposure to credit risk arising from 金等值及銀行存款產生的信貸風險有限，
cash and cash equivalents and deposits with banks is 因此，本集團認為此乃代表信貸風險低。
limited because the counterparties are major banks, for
which the Group considers to represent low credit risk.
Trade receivables and contract assets 應收賬款及合約資產
Credit terms offered by the Group to customers are 本集團給予客戶的信貸期乃基於與客戶
based on individual commercial terms negotiated with 商訂的個別商業條款而定。信貸期通常為
customers. Credit periods generally range from one day 一天至一個月。
to one month.

comparability of information about an entity’s financial 務報表資料的透明性及可比較性。《香港財務
statements. HKFRS 18 is effective for annual reporting periods 報告準則》第18號於二零二七年一月一日或之
beginning on or after 1 January 2027 and is to be applied 後開始的年度報告期間生效，並須追溯應用。
retrospectively.
Among other changes, under HKFRS 18, entities are required 除其他變動外，根據《香港財務報告準則》第18
to classify all income and expenses into five categories in the 號，實體須在損益表中將所有收入和支出分類
statement of profit or loss, namely the operating, investing, 為五個類別，即經營、投資、融資、已終止經
financing, discontinued operations and income tax categories. 營業務及所得稅類別。實體亦須在財務報表的
Entities are also required to provide specific disclosures about 單一附註中就管理層界定的績效指標提供具體
management-defined performance measures in a single note 披露。
in the financial statements.
The Group does not plan to early adopt HKFRS 18 and is still in 本集團計劃不會提前採用《香港財務報告準則》
the process of assessing the impact of the adoption. 第18號，目前仍在評估採用該準則的影響。
